# Third-party notices

Generated by `tools/license/gen-notices.sh`. Do not edit by hand.

Rust crates in the Android section are the Android runtime graph of `packages/rslib_bridge/rust` (`aarch64-linux-android`, `armv7-linux-androideabi`, `x86_64-linux-android`). Build-only and dev-only crates are omitted. Gradle dependencies are the Android `releaseRuntimeClasspath`. The desktop graph (`x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`, `aarch64-apple-darwin`) is 348 third-party crates in `apps/desktop/assets/licenses/rust-crates.json`.

Rust third-party crates: 345. Gradle dependencies: 46.

## Anki

Anki rslib is included unmodified from the third_party/anki submodule, commit 29bb700b951e3f0c0cb69b77c0180fc1fe33e6ba (tag 26.09.3). Copyright Ankitects Pty Ltd and contributors. License: AGPL-3.0-or-later, the same license as Hatto.

The path crates named with this entry are linked into the Android build. They are not ordinary third-party components. The notice is NOTICE.md.

anki_proto_gen is the same pin and the same license. The bridge build script uses it, and it is not linked into the APK.

Runtime path crates:

- anki 0.0.0
- anki_i18n 0.0.0
- anki_io 0.0.0
- anki_proto 0.0.0

## License choices

These runtime crates needed a choice or an `AND` of more than one grant. `OR` expressions are satisfied by the earliest license in `tools/license/about.toml`. GPL-2.0-only and LGPL-only are not accepted.

- encoding_rs 0.8.42: `(Apache-2.0 OR MIT) AND BSD-3-Clause` satisfied by Apache-2.0 AND BSD-3-Clause
- matchit 0.8.4: `MIT AND BSD-3-Clause` satisfied by BSD-3-Clause AND MIT
- priority-queue 2.7.0: `LGPL-3.0-or-later OR MPL-2.0` satisfied by MPL-2.0
- ring 0.17.14: `Apache-2.0 AND ISC` satisfied by Apache-2.0 AND ISC
- self_cell 1.3.0: `Apache-2.0 OR GPL-2.0-only` satisfied by Apache-2.0
- unicode-ident 1.0.27: `(MIT OR Apache-2.0) AND Unicode-3.0` satisfied by Apache-2.0 AND Unicode-3.0
- unicode-normalization 0.1.26: `(MIT OR Apache-2.0) AND Unicode-3.0` satisfied by Apache-2.0 AND Unicode-3.0

### Apache-2.0

- ring 0.17.14

```
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```

## Android Gradle dependencies

Collected from `releaseRuntimeClasspath` with `com.jaredsburrows.license` 0.9.91 (`generateJsonFullReport`). That plugin is Apache-2.0. Google's oss-licenses plugin is not used. The report is not copied into the APK by the build; the app loads `apps/mobile/assets/licenses/android-deps.json`.

`io.flutter` engine artifacts publish no license in their POM. Their text is the Flutter SDK `LICENSE` (BSD-3-Clause, Copyright 2014 The Flutter Authors). Third-party code linked into the engine (FreeType, HarfBuzz, ICU, and the rest) is in the Flutter `NOTICES` bundle, which the app reads through `LicenseRegistry`, and is not copied again here.

## Material Icons

`apps/mobile/pubspec.yaml` sets `uses-material-design: true`, so the release asset bundle contains `fonts/MaterialIcons-Regular.otf` from the Flutter SDK (`bin/cache/artifacts/material_fonts/`). The font's license file in that directory is CC BY 4.0, not Apache-2.0. Flutter's license collector reads package `LICENSE` / `NOTICES` files and does not read `MaterialIcons_LICENSE.txt`, so this text is registered with the app's `LicenseRegistry` from `material-icons.json`.

### CC-BY-4.0

- MaterialIcons-Regular.otf

## Desktop fonts

Golden tests for `apps/desktop` load Noto Sans Regular and Noto Sans KR Regular from `apps/desktop/test/fonts/` through `FontLoader`. Both are SIL Open Font License 1.1. The text below is `apps/desktop/test/fonts/OFL.txt`. The desktop app also loads `apps/desktop/assets/licenses/material-icons.json` and `apps/desktop/assets/licenses/flutter-sdk.json` (the Flutter SDK `LICENSE`, BSD-3-Clause). Engine third parties stay in Flutter's own `NOTICES` bundle via `LicenseRegistry`.

### OFL-1.1

- Noto Sans Regular
- Noto Sans KR Regular
